Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a statistical_mechanics algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 473. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 616/2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by statistical_mechanics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 473, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 616. QED.
